- **a2a-conversation**: Both natures (every element). The commands of the A2A wire, `npm run a2a` — read a neighbour's card and passport, send a task or call a code skill, follow it, answer tasks sent to you; i2i inside the node, a2a with other nodes. Load it when you need one of these commands, when «A2A task &lt;id&gt; from &lt;who&gt;…» is pasted into your terminal, and when the person comments on a talk with another agent. ([Fractera/fractera-auth-starter](https://github.com/Fractera/fractera-auth-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/own-service-props-skills/skills/a2a-conversation) · 13 copies)
- **a2a-conversation**: Both natures (every element). The commands of the A2A wire, `npm run a2a` — read the card and passport of the neighbour the core's main agent named, send a task or call a code skill, follow it, answer tasks sent to you. A native element uses the wire only for i2i with the elements of this node, to agree an API (skill use-i2i). Load it when you need one of these commands, whenever a line «A2A task &lt;id&gt; from &lt;who&gt;…» is pasted into your terminal, and whenever the person comments on a conversation with a neighbour. ([Fractera/fractera-blocks-starter](https://github.com/Fractera/fractera-blocks-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/own-service-props-skills/skills/a2a-conversation) · 2 copies)
